user
严格复盘v029 OJ结果并只选择一个v030最小结构诊断点。只读，不修改文件、不访问网络。
当前父版：D:/C_data/EurakaOpts/contest_fused_moe_gemm/v029_default_warp_specialized_probe.py
也可明确要求从v028派生，但必须说明原因。

证据链：
- v024 threads128 + disable warp specialized + swizzle：OJ compile true，ThreadSync warning，约50秒后segfault exit11。
- v025 删除actual_rows外层if：同结果。
- v026 删除swizzle：同结果。
- v028 threads256 + disable warp specialized + no swizzle：OJ compile true，无ThreadSync warning，仍约49.5秒后segfault exit11。
- v029 threads256 + default裸jit + no swizzle：C500 smoke、小型非零、官方E16/E32/E64均PASS；OJ submission104058 compile true，ThreadSync warning恢复，约50.2秒后segfault exit11。
结论：ThreadSync warning、swizzle、actual_rows外if、threads128/256、disable/default warp-specialized都不是segfault主因。所有OJ崩溃时间签名高度一致。

共同结构仍是：一个T.Kernel中按expert_tile循环，先两次FC1 GEMM累加gate/up，elementwise写up_activated_shared，再复制到up_activated_local，随后FC2 GEMM累加out_local；一个program block同时持有gate_local、up_local、up_activated_local、out_local和64KiB shared。
官方参考使用同一prim_func里的两个顺序T.Kernel launch，并通过外部up_logits workspace连接；它不会把FC1/FC2 fragments同时置于同一program block生命周期。但十参数接口没有workspace参数，不允许torch分配workspace，也不应假设T.alloc_global可用。

现在只选一个结构诊断，约束：
- 不能再改decorator、线程、swizzle、actual_rows或output store。
- 不允许引入外部参数或torch。
- 每版只能一个清晰改动点。
候选方向请评估后只批准一个：
A 删除`up_activated_shared`，直接在elementwise循环写`up_activated_local`，隔离shared-to-fragment桥接/生命周期；这同时减少16KiB shared，但fragment总量不变。
B 删除`up_activated_local`，尝试FC2直接以`up_activated_shared`作为A operand（如果TileLang GEMM layout合法），隔离fragment复制并减fragment资源。
C 通过复用gate_local或up_local承载fp16 activation（若dtype/layout允许），减少fragment生命周期，但必须避免数学/类型不合法。
D 其他一个更有证据的单改动，但不能拆双kernel，除非能严格说明十参数内合法workspace。

输出：OJ_EVIDENCE；ROOT_CAUSE_NARROWING；M1_SELECTED；WHY_OVER_ALTERNATIVES；EXACT_DIFF；LAYOUT_DTYPE_VALIDITY；RESOURCE_EFFECT；MATH_INTERFACE_COMPLIANCE；STATIC_ACCEPTANCE；SERVER_TESTS；OJ_EXPECTATION；GATE PASS_TO_IMPLEMENT/BLOCKED；BLOCKERS。
不允许把假设写成已证实。目标是选择下一步最有辨识力且可编译的单变量实验。

import math

import tilelang
import tilelang.language as T


_KERNEL_CACHE = {}


@tilelang.jit
def fused_moe_single_kernel(
    d_hidden,
    d_expert,
    n_routed_experts,
    padded_group_sum,
    raw_group_sum,
    group_count,
    num_blocks_m,
    block_token=128,
    block_hidden=128,
    block_expert=64,
    threads=256,
    num_stages=1,
):
    dtype = T.float16
    accum_dtype = T.float32
    scale = 1.44269504

    input_shape = (padded_group_sum, d_hidden)
    gate_shape = (n_routed_experts, d_expert, d_hidden)
    up_shape = (n_routed_experts, d_expert, d_hidden)
    down_shape = (n_routed_experts, d_hidden, d_expert)
    weights_shape = (raw_group_sum,)
    groups_shape = (group_count,)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor(input_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
        gate_w: T.Tensor(gate_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
        up_w: T.Tensor(up_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
        down_w: T.Tensor(down_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((raw_group_sum,), dtype),  # type: ignore
        group_sizes: T.Tensor(groups_shape, T.int32),  # type: ignore
        group_offsets: T.Tensor(groups_shape, T.int32),  # type: ignore
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor(groups_shape, T.int32),  # type: ignore
        group_idx_for_bx: T.Tensor((num_blocks_m,), T.int32),  # type: ignore
        out: T.Tensor(input_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
    ):
        with T.Kernel(num_blocks_m, T.ceildiv(d_hidden, block_hidden), threads=threads) as (bx, by):
            input_shared = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_hidden), dtype=dtype)
            gate_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_expert, block_hidden), dtype=dtype)
            up_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_expert, block_hidden), dtype=dtype)
            down_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_hidden, block_expert), dtype=dtype)
            up_activated_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_token, block_expert), dtype=dtype)
            gate_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_expert), dtype=accum_dtype)
            up_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_expert), dtype=accum_dtype)
            up_activated_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_expert), dtype=dtype)
            out_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_hidden), dtype=accum_dtype)

            expert_id = group_idx_for_bx[bx]
            padded_start = bx * block_token
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id] + padded_start - group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            token_offset = padded_start - group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            actual_rows = T.max(0, T.min(block_token, group_size - token_offset))

            T.clear(out_local)
            for expert_tile in T.Pipelined(T.ceildiv(d_expert, block_expert), num_stages=num_stages):
                T.clear(gate_local)
                T.clear(up_local)
                for hidden_tile in T.Pipelined(T.ceildiv(d_hidden, block_hidden), num_stages=num_stages):
                    for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_hidden):
                        hidden_col = hidden_tile * block_hidden + j
                        if (i < actual_rows) & (hidden_col < d_hidden):
                            input_shared[i, j] = stacked_expert_tokens[
                                padded_start + i,
                                hidden_col,
                            ]
                        else:
                            input_shared[i, j] = 0
                    for j in T.Parallel(block_hidden):
                        for i in T.Serial(block_expert):
                            expert_col = expert_tile * block_expert + i
                            hidden_col = hidden_tile * block_hidden + j
                            in_bounds = (expert_col < d_expert) & (hidden_col < d_hidden)
                            gate_shared[i, j] = T.if_then_else(
                                in_bounds,
                                gate_w[expert_id, expert_col, hidden_col],
                                0,
                            )
                            up_shared[i, j] = T.if_then_else(
                                in_bounds,
                                up_w[expert_id, expert_col, hidden_col],
                                0,
                            )
                    T.gemm(input_shared, gate_shared, gate_local, transpose_B=True)
                    T.gemm(input_shared, up_shared, up_local, transpose_B=True)

                for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_expert):
                    gate_value = gate_local[i, j]
                    gate_local[i, j] = gate_value * (1.0 / (1.0 + T.exp2(-gate_value * scale)))
                    up_activated_shared[i, j] = up_local[i, j] * gate_local[i, j]

                for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_expert):
                    up_activated_local[i, j] = up_activated_shared[i, j]

                for i in T.Parallel(block_hidden):
                    for j in T.Serial(block_expert):
                        hidden_col = by * block_hidden + i
                        expert_col = expert_tile * block_expert + j
                        down_shared[i, j] = T.if_then_else(
                            (hidden_col < d_hidden) & (expert_col < d_expert),
                            down_w[expert_id, hidden_col, expert_col],
                            0,
                        )
                T.gemm(up_activated_local, down_shared, out_local, transpose_B=True)
            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_hidden):
                if i < actual_rows:
                    out_local[i, j] = (
                        out_local[i, j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
                    )
                else:
                    out_local[i, j] = 0
            T.copy(
                out_local,
                out[
                    padded_start : padded_start + block_token,
                    by * block_hidden : (by + 1) * block_hidden,
                ],
            )

    return kernel


def run_kernel(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    gate_w,
    up_w,
    down_w,
    routed_expert_weights,
    group_sizes,
    group_offsets,
    group_padded_offsets,
    group_idx_for_bx,
    out,
):
    d_hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    d_expert = int(gate_w.shape[1])
    n_routed_experts = int(gate_w.shape[0])
    padded_group_sum = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0])
    raw_group_sum = int(routed_expert_weights.shape[0])
    group_count = int(group_sizes.shape[0])
    num_blocks_m = int(group_idx_for_bx.shape[0])
    key = (d_hidden, d_expert, n_routed_experts, padded_group_sum, raw_group_sum, group_count, num_blocks_m)
    kernel = _KERNEL_CACHE.get(key)
    if kernel is None:
        kernel = fused_moe_single_kernel(
            d_hidden=d_hidden,
            d_expert=d_expert,
            n_routed_experts=n_routed_experts,
            padded_group_sum=padded_group_sum,
            raw_group_sum=raw_group_sum,
            group_count=group_count,
            num_blocks_m=num_blocks_m,
        )
        _KERNEL_CACHE[key] = kernel
    kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        gate_w,
        up_w,
        down_w,
        routed_expert_weights,
        group_sizes,
        group_offsets,
        group_padded_offsets,
        group_idx_for_bx,
        out,
    )

D:\C_data\EurakaOpts\contest_fused_moe_gemm\problem_snapshot.json:50:        "text": "```python\nimport torch\n\ndef run_kernel(\n    stacked_expert_tokens: torch.Tensor,\n    gate_w: torch.Tensor,\n int(group_sizes[expert_idx].item())\n        if valid_count == 0:\n            continue\n\n        raw_start = int(group_offsets[expert_idx].item())\n        padded_start = int(group_padded_offsets[expert_idx].item())\n\n        x_e = x_f32[padded_start:padded_start + valid_count]\n        gate_logits = torch.matmul(x_e, gate_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        up_logits = torch.matmul(x_e, up_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        hidden_act = torch.sigmoid(gate_logits) * gate_logits * up_logits\n        y_e = torch.matmul(hidden_act, down_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        y_e *= routed_expert_weights[raw_start:raw_start + valid_count].to(torch.float32).unsqueeze(1)\n\n        out[padded_start:padded_start + valid_count].copy_(y_e.to(torch.float16))\n```\n\n这份参考实现对应的数学语义是：\n\n```text\nfor each expert e:\n    x_e = stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start : padded_start + valid_count]\n\n    gate_logits = x_e @ gate_w[e]^T\n    up_logits   = x_e @ up_w[e]^T\n    hidden      = silu(gate_logits) * up_logits\n    y_e         = hidden @ down_w[e]^T\n    y_e        *= routed_expert_weights[raw_start : raw_start + valid_count]\n\n    out[padded_start : padded_start + valid_count] = y_e\n```\n\n其中：\n\n- `raw_start = group_offsets[e]`\n- `padded_start = group_padded_offsets[e]`\n- `valid_count = group_sizes[e]`\n\n实现要点：\n\n**该题目的 GPU 计算算子不能使用 PyTorch，请只用 TileLang 完成相关计算算子。**  \n\n1. `group_offsets` 和 `group_padded_offsets` 需要分别使用，不能混用。\n2. `routed_expert_weights` 的索引基于真实 token 顺序。\n3. `stacked_expert_tokens` 与 `out` 的索引基于 padding 后顺序。\n4. padding 行不参与计算，输出保持为 `0`。\n5. 当前 baseline 主路径会把 `group_idx_for_bx` 用作 block 到 expert group 的映射。\n6. 为了对齐 TileLang kernel 的常见实现，最终结果按 `float16` 写回。\n\n"

D:\C_data\EurakaOpts\contest_fused_moe_gemm\v027_codex_diagnosis.txt:288:D:/C_data/EurakaOpts/contest_fused_moe_gemm\problem_snapshot.json:50:        "text": "```python\nimport torch\n\ndef run_kernel(\n    stacked_expert_tokens: torch.Tensor,\n    gate_w: torch.Tensor,\n    up_w: torch.Tensor,\n    down_w: torch.Tensor,\n    routed_expert_weights: torch.Tensor,\n    group_sizes: torch.Tensor,\n    group_offsets: torch.Tensor,\n    group_padded_offsets: torch.Tensor,\n    group_idx_for_bx: torch.Tensor,\n    out: torch.Tensor,\n) -> None:\n    out.zero_()\n    num_experts = int(group_sizes.numel())\n\n    x_f32 = stacked_expert_tokens.to(torch.float32)\n    gate_f32 = gate_w.to(torch.float32)\n    up_f32 = up_w.to(torch.float32)\n    down_f32 = down_w.to(torch.float32)\n\n    for expert_idx in range(num_experts):\n        valid_count = int(group_sizes[expert_idx].item())\n        if valid_count == 0:\n            continue\n\n        raw_start = int(group_offsets[expert_idx].item())\n        padded_start = int(group_padded_offsets[expert_idx].item())\n\n        x_e = x_f32[padded_start:padded_start + valid_count]\n        gate_logits = torch.matmul(x_e, gate_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        up_logits = torch.matmul(x_e, up_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        hidden_act = torch.sigmoid(gate_logits) * gate_logits * up_logits\n        y_e = torch.matmul(hidden_act, down_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        y_e *= routed_expert_weights[raw_start:raw_start + valid_count].to(torch.float32).unsqueeze(1)\n\n        out[padded_start:padded_start + valid_count].copy_(y_e.to(torch.float16))\n```\n\n这份参考实现对应的数学语义是：\n\n```text\nfor each expert e:\n    x_e = stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start : padded_start + valid_count]\n\n    gate_logits = x_e @ gate_w[e]^T\n    up_logits   = x_e @ up_w[e]^T\n    hidden      = silu(gate_logits) * up_logits\n    y_e         = hidden @ down_w[e]^T\n    y_e        *= routed_expert_weights[raw_start : raw_start + valid_count]\n\n    out[padded_start : padded_start + valid_count] = y_e\n```\n\n其中：\n\n- `raw_start = group_offsets[e]`\n- `padded_start = group_padded_offsets[e]`\n- `valid_count = group_sizes[e]`\n\n实现要点：\n\n**该题目的 GPU 计算算子不能使用 PyTorch，请只用 TileLang 完成相关计算算子。**  \n\n1. `group_offsets` 和 `group_padded_offsets` 需要分别使用，不能混用。\n2. `routed_expert_weights` 的索引基于真实 token 顺序。\n3. `stacked_expert_tokens` 与 `out` 的索引基于 padding 后顺序。\n4. padding 行不参与计算，输出保持为 `0`。\n5. 当前 baseline 主路径会把 `group_idx_for_bx` 用作 block 到 expert group 的映射。\n6. 为了对齐 TileLang kernel 的常见实现，最终结果按 `float16` 写回。\n\n"

block_hidden=128,\n    block_expert=64,\n    threads=128,\n    num_stages=1,\n):\n    dtype = T.float16\n    accum_dtype = T.float32\n    scale = 1.44269504\n\n    input_shape = (padded_group_sum, T.Tensor((raw_group_sum,), dtype),  # type: ignore\n        group_sizes: T.Tensor(groups_shape, T.int32),  # type: ignore\n        group_offsets: T.Tensor(groups_shape, T.int32),  # type: ignore\n        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor(groups_shape, T.int32),  # type: ignore\n        group_idx_for_bx: T.Tensor((num_blocks_m,), T.int32),  # type: ignore\n        out: T.Tensor(input_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore\n    ):\n        with T.Kernel(num_blocks_m, T.ceildiv(d_hidden, block_hidden), threads=threads) as (bx, by):\n            input_shared = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_hidden), down_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_hidden, block_expert), dtype=dtype)\n            up_activated_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_token, block_expert), dtype=dtype)\n            gate_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_expert), dtype=accum_dtype)\n            up_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_expert), dtype=accum_dtype)\n            up_activated_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_expert), dtype=dtype)\n            out_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_hidden), dtype=accum_dtype)\n\n            T.use_swizzle(10)\n            expert_id = group_idx_for_bx[bx]\n            padded_start = bx * block_token\n            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id] + padded_start - group_padded_offsets[expert_id]\n            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]\n            token_offset = padded_start - group_padded_offsets[expert_id]\n            actual_rows = T.max(0, T.min(block_token, group_size - token_offset))\n\n            T.clear(out_local)\n            if actual_rows > 0:\n                for expert_tile in T.Pipelined(T.ceildiv(d_expert, block_expert), num_stages=num_stages):\n i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_hidden):\n                            hidden_col = hidden_tile * block_hidden + j\n                            if (i < actual_rows) & (hidden_col < d_hidden):\n                                input_shared[i, j] = stacked_expert_tokens[\n                                    padded_start + i,\n                                    hidden_col,\n                                ]\n                            else:\n                                input_shared[i, j] = 0\n                        for j in T.Parallel(block_hidden):\n                            for i in T.Serial(block_expert):\n                                expert_col = expert_tile * block_expert + i\n                                hidden_col = hidden_tile * block_hidden + j\n                                in_bounds = (expert_col < d_expert) & (hidden_col < d_hidden)\n                                gate_shared[i, j] = T.if_then_else(\n                                    in_bounds,\n                                    gate_w[expert_id, expert_col, hidden_col],\n                                    0,\n                                )\n                                up_shared[i, j] = T.if_then_else(\n                                    in_bounds,\n                                    up_w[expert_id, expert_col, hidden_col],\n                                    0,\n                                )\n                        T.gemm(input_shared, gate_shared, gate_local, transpose_B=True)\n                        T.gemm(input_shared, up_shared, up_local, transpose_B=True)\n\n                    for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_expert):\n                        gate_value = gate_local[i, j]\n                        gate_local[i, j] = gate_value * (1.0 / (1.0 + T.exp2(-gate_value * scale)))\n                        up_activated_shared[i, j] = up_local[i, j] * gate_local[i, j]\n\n                    for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_expert):\n                        up_activated_local[i, j] = up_activated_shared[i, j]\n\n                    for i in T.Parallel(block_hidden):\n                        for j in T.Serial(block_expert):\n         )\n                    T.gemm(up_activated_local, down_shared, out_local, transpose_B=True)\n            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_hidden):\n                if i < actual_rows:\n                    out_local[i, j] = (\n                        out_local[i, j]\n                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]\n                    )\n                else:\n                    out_local[i, j] = 0\n            T.copy(\n                out_local,\n                out[\n                    padded_start : padded_start + block_token,\n                    by * block_hidden : (by + 1) * block_hidden,\n                ],\n            )\n\n    return kernel\n\n\ndef run_kernel(\n    stacked_expert_tokens,\n    gate_w,\n    up_w,\n    down_w,\n   32:     down_shape = (n_routed_experts, d_hidden, d_expert)
  33:     weights_shape = (raw_group_sum,)
  34:     groups_shape = (group_count,)
  35: 
  36:     @T.prim_func
  37:     def kernel(
  38:         stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor(input_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
  39:         gate_w: T.Tensor(gate_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
  40:         up_w: T.Tensor(up_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
  41:         down_w: T.Tensor(down_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
  42:         routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((raw_group_sum,), dtype),  # type: ignore
  43:         group_sizes: T.Tensor(groups_shape, T.int32),  # type: ignore
  44:         group_offsets: T.Tensor(groups_shape, T.int32),  # type: ignore
  45:         group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor(groups_shape, T.int32),  # type: ignore
  46:         group_idx_for_bx: T.Tensor((num_blocks_m,), T.int32),  # type: ignore
  47:         out: T.Tensor(input_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
  48:     ):
  49:         with T.Kernel(num_blocks_m, T.ceildiv(d_hidden, block_hidden), threads=threads) as (bx, by):
  50:             input_shared = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_hidden), dtype=dtype)
  51:             gate_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_expert, block_hidden), dtype=dtype)
  52:             up_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_expert, block_hidden), dtype=dtype)
  53:             down_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_hidden, block_expert), dtype=dtype)
  54:             up_activated_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_token, block_expert), dtype=dtype)
  55:             gate_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_expert), dtype=accum_dtype)
  56:             up_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_expert), dtype=accum_dtype)
  57:             up_activated_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_expert), dtype=dtype)
  58:             out_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_hidden), dtype=accum_dtype)
  59: 
  60:             expert_id = group_idx_for_bx[bx]
  61:             padded_start = bx * block_token
  62:             raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id] + padded_start - group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
  63:             group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
  64:             token_offset = padded_start - group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
  65:             actual_rows = T.max(0, T.min(block_token, group_size - token_offset))
  66: 
  67:             T.clear(out_local)
  68:             for expert_tile in T.Pipelined(T.ceildiv(d_expert, block_expert), num_stages=num_stages):
  69:                 T.clear(gate_local)
  70:                 T.clear(up_local)
  71:                 for hidden_tile in T.Pipelined(T.ceildiv(d_hidden, block_hidden), num_stages=num_stages):
  72:                     for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_hidden):
  73:                         hidden_col = hidden_tile * block_hidden + j
  74:                         if (i < actual_rows) & (hidden_col < d_hidden):
  75:                             input_shared[i, j] = stacked_expert_tokens[
  76:                                 padded_start + i,
  77:                                 hidden_col,
  78:                             ]
  79:                         else:
  80:                             input_shared[i, j] = 0
  81:                     for j in T.Parallel(block_hidden):
  82:                         for i in T.Serial(block_expert):
  83:                             expert_col = expert_tile * block_expert + i
  84:                             hidden_col = hidden_tile * block_hidden + j
  85:                             in_bounds = (expert_col < d_expert) & (hidden_col < d_hidden)
  86:                             gate_shared[i, j] = T.if_then_else(
  87:                                 in_bounds,
  88:                                 gate_w[expert_id, expert_col, hidden_col],
  89:                                 0,
  90:                             )
  91:                             up_shared[i, j] = T.if_then_else(
  92:                                 in_bounds,
  93:                                 up_w[expert_id, expert_col, hidden_col],
  94:                                 0,
  95:                             )
  96:                     T.gemm(input_shared, gate_shared, gate_local, transpose_B=True)
  97:                     T.gemm(input_shared, up_shared, up_local, transpose_B=True)
  98: 
  99:                 for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_expert):
 100:                     gate_value = gate_local[i, j]
 101:                     gate_local[i, j] = gate_value * (1.0 / (1.0 + T.exp2(-gate_value * scale)))
 102:                     up_activated_shared[i, j] = up_local[i, j] * gate_local[i, j]
 103: 
 104:                 for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_expert):
 105:                     up_activated_local[i, j] = up_activated_shared[i, j]
 106: 
 107:                 for i in T.Parallel(block_hidden):
 108:                     for j in T.Serial(block_expert):
 109:                         hidden_col = by * block_hidden + i
 110:                         expert_col = expert_tile * block_expert + j
 111:                         down_shared[i, j] = T.if_then_else(
 112:                             (hidden_col < d_hidden) & (expert_col < d_expert),
 113:                             down_w[expert_id, hidden_col, expert_col],
 114:                             0,
 115:                         )
 116:                 T.gemm(up_activated_local, down_shared, out_local, transpose_B=True)
 117:             for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_hidden):
 118:                 if i < actual_rows:
 119:                     out_local[i, j] = (
 120:                         out_local[i, j]
 121:                         * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
 122:                     )
 123:                 else:
 124:                     out_local[i, j] = 0
 125:             T.copy(
 126:                 out_local,
 127:                 out[
 128:                     padded_start : padded_start + block_token,
 129:                     by * block_hidden : (by + 1) * block_hidden,
 130:                 ],
 131:             )
 132: 
 133:     return kernel
 134: 
 135: 
 136: def run_kernel(
 137:     stacked_expert_tokens,
 138:     gate_w,
 139:     up_w,
 140:     down_w,
 141:     routed_expert_weights,
 142:     group_sizes,
 143:     group_offsets,
 144:     group_padded_offsets,
 145:     group_idx_for_bx,
 146:     out,
 147: ):
 148:     d_hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
 149:     d_expert = int(gate_w.shape[1])
 150:     n_routed_experts = int(gate_w.shape[0])
 151:     padded_group_sum = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0])
 152:     raw_group_sum = int(routed_expert_weights.shape[0])
 153:     group_count = int(group_sizes.shape[0])
 154:     num_blocks_m = int(group_idx_for_bx.shape[0])
 155:     key = (d_hidden, d_expert, n_routed_experts, padded_group_sum, raw_group_sum, group_count, num_blocks_m)
 156:     kernel = _KERNEL_CACHE.get(key)
 157:     if kernel is None:
 158:         kernel = fused_moe_single_kernel(
 159:             d_hidden=d_hidden,
 160:             d_expert=d_expert,
 161:             n_routed_experts=n_routed_experts,
 162:             padded_group_sum=padded_group_sum,
 163:             raw_group_sum=raw_group_sum,
 164:             group_count=group_count,
 165:             num_blocks_m=num_blocks_m,
 166:         )
 167:         _KERNEL_CACHE[key] = kernel
 168:     kernel(
 169:         stacked_expert_tokens,
 170:         gate_w,
 171:         up_w,
 172:         down_w,
 173:         routed_expert_weights,
 174:         group_sizes,
 175:         group_offsets,
 176:         group_padded_offsets,
 177:         group_idx_for_bx,
 178:         out,
 179:     )

codex
候选 A 的关键优势是保留 FC2 的 `T.gemm(up_activated_local, down_shared, ...)` 形态不变，只改 activation 的生产路径；这让它比 B/C 更像单变量诊断，而不是同时试探 GEMM operand 合法性或 dtype 复用。
codex
OJ_EVIDENCE  
v029 OJ submission `104058`: compile `success: true`，status `RuntimeError`，testcase time `50159302`，memory `23407096`，`ThreadSync` warning 恢复，systemMessage `Exit code: 11`。  
v028 OJ submission `104027`: compile `success: true`，无 `ThreadSync` warning，testcase time `49459490`，memory `23407244`，systemMessage `Exit code: 11`。  
v028 -> v029 diff 只是一行 decorator：禁用 warp-specialized 改为裸 `@tilelang.jit`。因此 v029 不能把崩溃主因归因到 decorator 或 warning。

ROOT_CAUSE_NARROWING  
已被弱化或排除为主因的变量：`ThreadSync warning`、swizzle、`actual_rows` 外层 if、threads 128/256、disable/default warp-specialized。  
仍共同存在的高风险结构是：单个 program block 在同一 `T.Kernel` 生命周期内同时保留 FC1 的 `gate_local/up_local`、activation fragment、FC2 的 `out_local`，并有 `up_activated_shared` 作为 shared-to-fragment 桥接。  
这只是当前最窄可疑结构，不是已证实 root cause。

M1_SELECTED  
选择 A：删除 `up_activated_shared`，直接在 elementwise 循环写 `up_activated_local`。

WHY_OVER_ALTERNATIVES  
A 保持 FC2 GEMM operand 形态不变：仍是 `T.gemm(up_activated_local, down_shared, out_local, transpose_B=True)`，所以主要隔离 shared activation bridge/lifetime。  
B 会同时测试 `alloc_shared((block_token, block_expert), fp16)` 作为 GEMM A operand 是否被 TileLang/C500 合法接受，变量不干净，编译风险更高。  
C 复用 `gate_local/up_local` 会碰到 dtype/layout 问题：它们是 fp32 accum fragment，而 FC2 A operand 需要 fp16 activation fragment；改它会把数学、dtype、fragment 生命周期混在一起。  
D 暂不选；双 kernel 需要外部 workspace 或可靠的 kernel 内 global workspace 说明，当前十参数和禁止 torch 条件下不能作为最小合法诊断。

EXACT_DIFF  
基于 [v029_default_warp_specialized_probe.py](D:/C_data/EurakaOpts/contest_fused_moe_gemm/v029_default_warp_specialized_probe.py:54)：

```diff
-            up_activated_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_token, block_expert), dtype=dtype)
             gate_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_expert), dtype=accum_dtype)
             up_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_expert), dtype=accum_dtype)
             up_activated_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_expert), dtype=dtype)

             for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_expert):
                 gate_value = gate_local[i, j]
                 gate_local[i, j] = gate_value * (1.0 / (1.0 + T.exp2(-gate_value * scale)))
-                up_activated_shared[i, j] = up_local[i, j] * gate_local[i, j]
-
-            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_expert):
-                up_activated_local[i, j] = up_activated_shared[i, j]
+                up_activated_local[i, j] = up_local[i, j] * gate_local[i, j]
```

LAYOUT_DTYPE_VALIDITY  
预期合法，但需编译验证。`up_activated_local` 已是 fp16 fragment，原本就是 FC2 GEMM 的 A operand；A 只改变它的写入来源。fp32 表达式写入 fp16 fragment会发生原实现 shared fp16 写入时已经存在的同类降精度路径，不引入新的数学 dtype 目标。

RESOURCE_EFFECT  
shared memory 减少 `128 * 64 * 2 = 16384 bytes`。  
fragment 总量不变：`gate_local fp32`、`up_local fp32`、`up_activated_local fp16`、`out_local fp32` 仍同时存在。  
删除一个 `T.Parallel(block_token, block_expert)` copy loop，减少一次 shared 写和一次 shared 读。

MATH_INTERFACE_COMPLIANCE  
数学不变：`up_activated_local = up_local * silu(gate_local)`，随后 down GEMM 和 routed weight scale 不变。  
十参数 `run_kernel` 不变；不引入 torch、不引入外部 workspace、不改 output store。

STATIC_ACCEPTANCE  
静态上 PASS：不触碰 decorator、threads、swizzle、`actual_rows`、output store、接口和缓存键。  
但这不是编译已证实，只是最小源码变更可接受。

SERVER_TESTS  
v030 必须至少跑：`py_compile`、禁用 API grep、C500 smoke、小型非零 correctness、官方 E16/E32/E64 correctness、padding/input immutability、repeat deterministic。  
若编译失败，结论限定为“direct fragment write 不被当前 TileLang lowering 接受”。

OJ_EXPECTATION  
若 OJ 仍约 50 秒 exit11：shared activation bridge 不是主因，焦点继续收窄到单 block 持有的 fragment 生命周期/资源压力或 fused FC1+FC2 结构本身。  
若 warning/segfault 变化：A 对 shared lifetime 或 lowering 有辨识力。  
不能预期它必然修复 OJ；它是诊断点。

GATE PASS_TO_IMPLEMENT

BLOCKERS  
无静态 blocker。唯一风险是服务器编译/后端 lowering 未验证。
tokens used
32,113
OJ_EVIDENCE  
v029 OJ submission `104058`: compile `success: true`，status `RuntimeError`，testcase time `50159302`，memory `23407096`，`ThreadSync` warning 恢复，systemMessage `Exit code: 11`。  
v028 OJ submission `104027`: compile `success: true`，无 `ThreadSync` warning，testcase time `49459490`，memory `23407244`，systemMessage `Exit code: 11`。  
v028 -> v029 diff 只是一行 decorator：禁用 warp-specialized 改为裸 `@tilelang.jit`。因此 v029 不能把崩溃主因归因到 decorator 或 warning。

ROOT_CAUSE_NARROWING  
已被弱化或排除为主因的变量：`ThreadSync warning`、swizzle、`actual_rows` 外层 if、threads 128/256、disable/default warp-specialized。  
仍共同存在的高风险结构是：单个 program block 在同一 `T.Kernel` 生命周期内同时保留 FC1 的 `gate_local/up_local`、activation fragment、FC2 的 `out_local`，并有 `up_activated_shared` 作为 shared-to-fragment 桥接。  
这只是当前最窄可疑结构，不是已证实 root cause。

M1_SELECTED  
选择 A：删除 `up_activated_shared`，直接在 elementwise 循环写 `up_activated_local`。

WHY_OVER_ALTERNATIVES  
A 保持 FC2 GEMM operand 形态不变：仍是 `T.gemm(up_activated_local, down_shared, out_local, transpose_B=True)`，所以主要隔离 shared activation bridge/lifetime。  
B 会同时测试 `alloc_shared((block_token, block_expert), fp16)` 作为 GEMM A operand 是否被 TileLang/C500 合法接受，变量不干净，编译风险更高。  
C 复用 `gate_local/up_local` 会碰到 dtype/layout 问题：它们是 fp32 accum fragment，而 FC2 A operand 需要 fp16 activation fragment；改它会把数学、dtype、fragment 生命周期混在一起。  
D 暂不选；双 kernel 需要外部 workspace 或可靠的 kernel 内 global workspace 说明，当前十参数和禁止 torch 条件下不能作为最小合法诊断。

EXACT_DIFF  
基于 [v029_default_warp_specialized_probe.py](D:/C_data/EurakaOpts/contest_fused_moe_gemm/v029_default_warp_specialized_probe.py:54)：

```diff
-            up_activated_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_token, block_expert), dtype=dtype)
             gate_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_expert), dtype=accum_dtype)
             up_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_expert), dtype=accum_dtype)
             up_activated_local = T.alloc_fragment((block_token, block_expert), dtype=dtype)

             for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_expert):
                 gate_value = gate_local[i, j]
                 gate_local[i, j] = gate_value * (1.0 / (1.0 + T.exp2(-gate_value * scale)))
-                up_activated_shared[i, j] = up_local[i, j] * gate_local[i, j]
-
-            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_expert):
-                up_activated_local[i, j] = up_activated_shared[i, j]
+                up_activated_local[i, j] = up_local[i, j] * gate_local[i, j]
```

LAYOUT_DTYPE_VALIDITY  
预期合法，但需编译验证。`up_activated_local` 已是 fp16 fragment，原本就是 FC2 GEMM 的 A operand；A 只改变它的写入来源。fp32 表达式写入 fp16 fragment会发生原实现 shared fp16 写入时已经存在的同类降精度路径，不引入新的数学 dtype 目标。

RESOURCE_EFFECT  
shared memory 减少 `128 * 64 * 2 = 16384 bytes`。  
fragment 总量不变：`gate_local fp32`、`up_local fp32`、`up_activated_local fp16`、`out_local fp32` 仍同时存在。  
删除一个 `T.Parallel(block_token, block_expert)` copy loop，减少一次 shared 写和一次 shared 读。

MATH_INTERFACE_COMPLIANCE

十参数 `run_kernel` 不变；不引入 torch、不引入外部 workspace、不改 output store。

STATIC_ACCEPTANCE  
静态上 PASS：不触碰 decorator、threads、swizzle、`actual_rows`、output store、接口和缓存键。
